## Fixes2 (2026-10-28)

**Fixes3 changes (same day):** `WorldText.PadLabel` has `Title` and `Price` (TycoonService fills them; "$" prices); `WorldText.BuyButton` carries a `Label` (Title, Price) over the button, filled by TycoonController from the pad's label, which it hides while you stand there. `WorldText.OreLabel` fixed text sizes, FixedSize strokes. Guide: `drawTrail(points, glide)` runs every RenderStepped on `routePoints` (SPEED 4, fades at both ends). ShopService `FIRST_LETTER_DELAY` 3. WeatherGui: one `Icon` (ImageButton, bottom-left, image ids as attributes) with a tap `Tip`; WeatherController's roof test uses `InsideZone`s; `WeatherFx.Rain.Drops` uses the rain streak texture.

- **Tutorial's end:** the plot attribute `TutorialDone` (true once the first letter is sent): TycoonService's `onPrepare` sets it from the save, ShopService when the letter is sent, DevTools' Skip tutorial. Read by ShopService (`TUTORIAL_GAP`, VIPs, Snow), ForgeService (Rain, Auto Ore Roller), `MyPlot.tutorialDone` (Guide, MenuController, WeatherController) and GemController. Replaces `Bought_OreStand2` or `Merges` > 0.
- `Guide`: `tutorialDone` also counts the first letter `done` in your state; no re-pull step or Ore Slot 2 step; `mailButton` returns `Content.Letter` while its button shows; `closedAfterAccept`; the Gem Shop step spotlights the row. `GuideLook.Arrow.TextureSpeed` 0.
- `BladeCore.EARLY_FINISH_SHARE` (0.75) and `canFinish(blade, state)`; `ForgeCheck.evaluate` accepts from `canFinish`, grade `forPercent(100 × strikes/recipe)`, multiplier `MAX_MULTIPLIER × strikes/recipe`. `BladeData.blank` is the ingot bar (re-running the tanto-forge exporter would undo it). `AnvilController`: `finishForging` (mode `"finishing"`), Finish enabled at `ready` + `canFinish`; `AnvilHud.Bottom.Finish` has no UIGradient/Glow.
- `ServerScriptService.Weather`: `WEATHERS` (weight + effect attribute), `SPELL` 300 s, `start(w)` for a later bought weather; ReplicatedStorage attributes `Weather`, `WeatherEnds`, `WeatherCustomers`, `WeatherMail`, `WeatherLuck`, `WeatherVip`. Read by ShopService (walk-in gap, mail clock, `Customers.pick(rng, vipChance)`) and `ForgeService.rollFor` (luck). `Remotes.DevWeather` (was DevSunshine). `WeatherController` clones `ReplicatedStorage.WeatherFx` (Rain, Snow, Rainbow) to the camera (rain/snow off under a roof) and fades `Lighting.RainGrade`/`SnowGrade`/`RainbowGrade`/`SunshineGrade`; `WeatherGui` chips Sunny/Snow/Rain/Rainbow (`Bonus` attribute).
- `ShopService.makeNpc` clones `WorldText.VIPHighlight` onto VIP customers.
- `WorldText.BuyButton` 132×48 px, `StudsOffset` y 1.5; `WorldText.OreLabel` 110×42 px, `MaxDistance` 16; `WorldText.PadLabel` stroke 2, (20,84,24). MailGui Letter's `Want` row at y −54 above `Buttons`; MailGui.Popup 64 px tall.
- Shop-front `Noren`/`NorenRod` gone from `Plot.Shop.Starter/Upgraded.Shell` (in `ServerStorage.Legacy.Fixes2_1028`); `Builders.build_shops` leaves the entrance bay open.

## Upgrade order (2026-10-28)

[[Archive/Design/Upgrade Order Review — 2026-10-28]], [[Sessions/2026-10-28#Upgrade order built]]. Built in Edit, not played.

- `ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Upgrades`: the spine Katana → Sword Crafter → Longsword → Anvil 3 (Haru) → Stocker → Falchion → Anvil 4 (Mei) → Shop Promoter → Gladius → Cashier → Sword Etcher → Zanbatō; two stages open (a stage's pads `after` the goal before the last one, Anvil 3 / Anvil 4 standing for the crafters); new prices (Katana 1,300 … Zanbatō 300,000; Ore Slots 3–6 280 / 2,000 / 9,800 / 20,000). The header says how it's gated.
- `ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Deliveries`: `CAPACITY_PRICE` 1,500 / 25,000 / 64,000, `SPEED_PRICE` 2,400 / 40,000 / 55,000 / 76,000; `CAPACITY_AFTER` { LongswordBlueprint, FalchionBlueprint }, `SPEED_AFTER` { Anvil3, Anvil4, Promoter }.
- `ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Employees`: crafter `after` (an upgrade id or a crafter id: Haru `Anvil3`, Mei `Anvil4`, Kenji `mei`, Rin `kenji`) and `Employees.hireOpen(c, plot, hired)`; crew coins 23,000 / 70,000 / 460,000 / 710,000; `TRAIN_PRICE` falchion 4,000, gladius 6,000 (only for a shape the workshop lacks).
- `ServerScriptService.EmployeeService`: in `PlotService.onPlot`, `teach(switch)` trains `crafter.trained` on every non-big blueprint you know; each blueprint pad's `Bought_<id>` change runs `teach(true)`, which sets `crafter.making` to the dearest known sword; `hire()` keeps `making = making or bestMade(d)` and then teaches; the `hire` remote action also needs `Employees.hireOpen`.
- `StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.EmployeeController`: requires `Shared.Upgrades`; a roster card whose `after` isn't met shows the veil and "<upgrade or crafter> Required" in `Needs`.
- `Development/economy_model.py` (vault): the built tree as `PADS`, `GATE` for cart levels and crew, merges, crew teaching, `rate()` for payback, `fit()` (cheapest-first stage nudging), `walls()`.

## Hilt quality pass (2026-10-29)

[[Sessions/2026-10-29#Hilt quality pass]]. Backups: `ServerStorage.Legacy.<Name>Hilt_beforeQualityPass`.

- `ReplicatedStorage.SwordParts.<Name>Hilt` for Cutlass, Rapier, Khopesh, Scimitar, Flamberge, Claymore, Longsword, Falchion, Gladius, HookSword, Dao: new Grip / Guard / Pommel meshes (same names, roles, properties and `GripLength` / `BladeOffset`), placed with the ten's rotation at (-z, -y, -x) of each part's Blender bounds centre (the Y term was missing from the fictional row above; it only matters off y = 0). `Back` 0.39 / 0.704 / 0.418 / 0.4541 / 1.089 / 1.177 / 0.959 / 0.635 / 0.558 / 1.165 / 0.91; `SourceAsset` in `Models/Hilts/Quality/assets.json`. The Flamberge's `Grip` also holds the leather sleeve over the ricasso (tier grip colour); the rapier's pas d'âne and the flamberge's lugs reach past `BladeOffset` over the blade.
- Built by `Models/Hilts/build_quality_hilts.py` (`QualityHilts.blend`); review blades from `Models/Hilts/Quality/blades.json` (Studio's finished `BladeCore` params); IoU against the silhouette sheet with `Quality/iou.py`; upload with `Quality/upload.py`. JianHilt, MacheteHilt and KatanaHilt unchanged.

| `Shared/EtchPatterns` (2026-10-29) | `strokes(id, length, widthAt, edgeAt?)`.
- `core(length, widthAt, edgeAt)`, cached per `widthAt`: samples every 0.5 cm, detrends by the mean midline, opens the edges (max then min over R = 1.5 × the median capped width, shrinking into the tip), then a half-radius erosion and mean. It returns the width and the lower side's height above the edge line.
- Generators see the core width capped at 7 cm.
- `limitOf` is the start of the last steep stretch before the tip.
- Points are clamped to the face with `room` (`0.035 w + r` to `0.965 w − r`, where r = THICK × h + halo). Fill polygons are clipped straight over their x-range, and shards with nothing left are dropped.
- `Shared/BladeEtch.surface().edgeAt(x)` is the edge line in cm. [[Sessions/2026-10-29#Fictional swords: even etches and symmetric markings]] |
| `Models/Hilts/build_fictional_hilts.py` (2026-10-29) | `paint_sym(name, bm_map, polys_uw, ...)`: (u, w) outlines off the blade's middle; the keep-side half is triangulated, reflected and clamped onto the blade. `mirror_halves(obj)`: bisects at x = 0, mirrors and merges. `trace_ref.py`: `symmetric(mask, ax, pair)`, and `cb` (the blade's centre column) in `traced.json`. |
| `Models/Swords/New Silhouettes/` (2026-11-10, Blender only, not in Studio) | 22 swords from `References/Sword Silhouettes/new-silhouettes-a-v1.png` / `-b-v1.png`. `crop.py` (miniconda python, cv2) -> `masks/<id>.png`, `cells.json`, `ruler.png`; `segment.py` + `cuts.json` ([blade_end, grip_top, grip_bot] rows) -> `parts/<id>.png` (1 blade, 2 guard, 3 grip, 4 pommel), `_groove.png`, `segment.json` (axis, cuts), `segment.png`; `prep.py <out>` -> per sword `.npz` (half-thickness maps, 2x) + `.json` (outline and bevel loops); `build_blender.py` in Blender (`SRC = <out>`; exec) -> `NewSilhouettes.blend`. `K` = 5/450 studs per px; blade 2.2 px half-thick with a 7 px bevel, guard 3.8 px, rim walls 0.35-1 px. v2 (same day): `prep.py` traces the sheet's grey per part (`distanceTransformWithLabels` nearest part), `smooth_loop` (Gaussian 0.9 px along the curve, held within 2 px of turns over `CORNER_DEG` 45), drops loops under 30 px², seats parts (`SEAT_BLADE` 8, `SEAT_GRIP` 5, `SEAT_POMMEL` 5 px, inside the closed silhouette) and keeps only the grip's height map; `build_blender.py` uses `inside()` (even-odd) and `dist_to()` (exact edge distance) for the grid, triangles and heights, and `SMOOTH_ITERS` 6 on guards and pommels; `outline_check.py <out>` draws every loop to `outline_check.png`. |

| `Shared/Deliveries` (2026-10-29) | Requires `Blueprints`. `mismatch(o, sword)` returns "Stand only" for a big blueprint before anything else, so no order takes one. `DeliveryService.refill` builds offers from known blueprints minus big ones. |

| `Shared/Orders` (2026-10-29) | `pickBlueprint(rng, known)`: the top three non-big blueprints by `basePrice`, weights 3:2:1. `Orders.typicalMetal(luck)` = Σ multiplier × `Materials.odds(luck)` / 100. `Orders.pay(value, metalMult?, typical?)` = value × max(1, typical / metalMult) × PAY. `ShopService` Order "fill" passes the sword's metal multiplier and `Upgrades.mergeFactor(d.merges)`. |

| `ServerStorage.Builders.ExportBalance` (2026-10-29) | Edit only. `(url?, mode?)`: posts `balance.json` and `golden.json` to `url` (default `http://127.0.0.1:8765`); with `mode == "hashes"` it returns the 22 sources' FNV-1a hashes. It requires a clone of `Shared` (`ExportShared`) and a SmithyCore clone pointed at it. Script constants are read by pattern (`grab`). Golden draws use `fake(seq)` (NextNumber in turn; NextInteger = a + floor(u·(b−a+1))). |
| `Development/sim/` (2026-10-29) | `rules.py` (`load()` → `Balance`: the game's formulas from `balance.json`; `FakeRng`), `test_parity.py` (unittest against `golden.json`), `check_export.py` (hash check), `receive.py` (localhost receiver). `economy_model.py` imports `rules` and takes every game number from the export. |

| `ShopService.browseEmpty(plot, ctx)` (2026-10-29) | `runCustomer` calls it when no holder of the plot has a sword. It picks a random unlocked slot (`slot.customer = ctx` while there), walks in along `slot.route` to its Inspect point, `say`s "Nothing for sale yet?", waits 2.5 s, and walks back out to `CustomerSpawn`. No rickshaw, no Promoter count. |
| `Development/sim/` step 2 (2026-10-29) | `engine.Sim` (heapq events: player, spawn, offer, answer, letter, crafter, stocker, etcher, weather, autoroll, cart_back, browsed, sample; `self.T` timings), `behavior.ARCHETYPES` / `PAYERS` / `choose_target`, `scenarios.SCENARIOS`, `run.py` (multiprocessing, seeds `<seed>:<i>`), `report.write(out_dir)`. Layout in `balance.json["layout"]`. |

| `ServerScriptService.PlaytestLog` (2026-10-29) | ModuleScript, Studio only: `log(player, kind, data)`. Batches and posts to `http://127.0.0.1:8765/playtest/<UTC start>` every 15 s and on BindToClose, keeping the events if that fails (max 50,000). Also logs `pos` every 1 s in plot space (`PlotService.plotOf(...):GetPivot()`), `prompt` / `hold` (`ProximityPromptService`, `<model>/<ActionText>`), `join` and `leave`. Hooks: ForgeService (`pull`, `collect` in `takeOre`, `furnace_put`, `furnace_take`, and the crafter `handBack` with `crafter = true`), SmithService (`forge_begin`, `forge_finish`), PolishService (`polish_begin`, `polish`), EtchService (`etch`), ShopService (`display`, `offer` in `askOwner`, `visit`, `browse`, `sale`) and TycoonService (`buy`). |

PlaytestLog adds `rack` (RackService.RackAction), `forge` on furnace events and `u` on `pos`. |

ServerStorage 99,241 → 3,661 instances; the place 153,922 → 54,315 script lines. `Legacy` stays for new backups, with `SharedV1` (AnvilSelfCheck). Restore: Insert from File. |
| `DeliveryService` (`dropRefill`) | 2026-11-02: cancelling the active or queued order counts as a skip: the newest offer (the one its accept refilled) becomes a `SKIP_WAIT` wait, so offers + waits stay at `Deliveries.OFFERS` (no wait is added when every slot already waits). Accept then cancel no longer re-rolls offers for free. |

It used to stay on the rack unseen. |

| `SwordRenderer` shop shine | 2026-11-03: `shine(model, part, path, polished)` for a displayed sword (Model, `Prop` ~= true), stopped in `free()`; the glare `glint` now runs only on held swords and props. One `RenderStepped` loop over `shines`: within `SHINE_NEAR` 35 and not hidden (`LocalTransparencyModifier` < 1) it builds up to `SHINE_PIECES` 64 `Beam`s (FaceCamera, colour 215,225,235, Brightness 1.1, 30% clear) between `ShineLine` attachments along `edgePath` (× the model's scale), else destroys them. Lit stretch `SHINE_HALF` 0.14 each side of `u`, width `SHINE_WIDTH` 0.06 with a smoothstep taper; `u` = 0.5 − 0.5·(view·axis)/`SHINE_SIDE` 0.8 + a random bias, strength from |view·UpVector|; both re-aim only after the camera moves `SHINE_STEP` 0.4, eased at `SHINE_EASE` 6. Polished: a pulse (`PULSE_HALF` 0.09, +0.07 width, +3 brightness) over `WAVE_TIME` every 2.5–4.5 s. Widths are rewritten only when `u`, strength or a pulse changed. [[Sessions/2026-11-03#Shop shine on displayed swords]] |

Touch aims `TOUCH_LIFT` 0.09 of the screen above the finger; `showRing` sizes and places `PolishHud.Brush` (circle Frame, white UIStroke). `padScreen(u, w)`; R2 zigzags `padW` over `PAD_ZIG` 0.45 s. | `EtchingTableController` | Module colour block: `etchColor` (kept between shows), `metalColor`, `onColor`, `paintColor`, `setColor`, `pickAt`, `dragTo` (mouse `GetMouseLocation`, touch `Position`), `toggleColors`, `stickStep` (0.12 s). `etchSword(sword, etch)` starts on `etchColor`, sets `onColor` (one `session:recolor` at a time, then the latest) once loaded; `drawn` clears `onColor` and sends `etchColor`. `play`: binds `EtchColors` (ButtonY) and `EtchColorStick` (Thumbstick2); button visible while `session.ready` and `onColor`; camera `slide` eases (dt x 12) toward centring the sword between the top bar and `Colors`. Pattern cards and `warmEtch` use `etchColor`. |

- **Polish sheen (2026-11-09):** `PolishController` grime: `SHEEN[row]` (alpha per strip row: 0.6·exp(-((w-0.62)/0.11)²) + 0.25·exp(-((w-0.2)/0.05)²), 0 under 0.004), `CLEAN[row]` (packed white + alpha for `writeu32`), `fillRun(rgba, at, n, value)`, `skin(rgba, base, i, row, dirt)` (alpha = dirt·0.85 + sheen·(1-dirt), colour from `base` toward white). `Grime.base` is the built skin before the sheen. `buildGrime` fills each same-polish run per row in `GRIME`'s plain colour; `wipe`, `clearGrime` (row fills of `CLEAN`) and `finishGlint`'s columns use it. `BladeMount.PolishLight.SpotLight.Color` rgb(235, 242, 255). Backup `Legacy.PolishController_beforeSheen_1109`.

## Blade holes (2026-11-10)

| Where | What |
| --- | --- |
| `Shared/BladeCore` | `HOLE_PARAMS` (`holeC`, `holeW`: centre and half-width as fractions from the spine), `HOLE` = 16. `sampleShape` reads a stage's `hole = { {u, a, b} }` (monotone curves for each side; zero width outside its first and last u). `createBlade` gives stages 1-3 the refined centre at zero width. `paramNames` / `ALL_PARAMS`: `copyParams`, `stationParams`, `lerpParams` carry the hole params when present. `hasHole(blade)`, `vertexCount(N, holed)`, `buildIndex(N, holed)`: a holed mesh keeps the ring's edge and mune faces (`HOLED_RING_FACES`) and draws its faces through `HOLE_STRIPS` (block after the caps: R1, R1', A, B, R2, R2' top, the same bottom, A and B walls). `buildPositions` writes the block when `params.holeW`; `HOLE_RING` is what `ringOfVertex` answers for it. `validateProfile`: "The hole stays inside the blade, with steel round it". [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Blades can have holes]] 2026-11-10: openings apart along the blade share the one hole (closed a == b between them); a refined stage with `fork = true` (`Shape.fork`) may reach the sides (a = 0 / b = 1), the rim check then 0 instead of 0.05: an open fork, the shorter prong narrowed to nothing on its own line (mesh unchanged). Backup `Legacy.BladeCore_beforeFork_1110`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#S05 fork]] |
| `Shared/BladeView`, `Shared/BladeProbe` | `vertexCount` / `buildIndex` with `BladeCore.hasHole(blade)`. |
| `Shared/BladeEtch` | `surface().holeAt(x)` -> the hole's sides as w (edge 0, spine 1; equal where closed), `surface().holeRange` (cm). `splitAtHole(cols, wOf, clamp, wa, wb)`: each column twice, clamped to each side; the `gap` span isn't drawn. `buildTextured`: `HOLE_ROWS` = 24 rows along the hole (0.08 cm minimum). `build` (Flame): rows capped at 500 on a holed blade (EditableMesh 20k triangles). |

## Repo audit fixes (2026-11-10)

- `Development/upload_asset.py`: `python -I Development/upload_asset.py <file> ...` → `{name: assetId}`; `CREATOR = {"groupId": "336242048"}`; `TYPES` maps `.fbx` / `.glb` to Model, `.png` / `.jpg` to Decal and `.mp3` / `.ogg` to Audio (2026-11-11); `upload(path)` raises on failure; each id goes to stderr as it's ready; the exit code is 1 if any file failed. Replaces every per-folder `upload*.py`. Animations (.rbxm) aren't covered. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Repo audit fixes]]
- `Development/Dialogue Editor/server.js`: 403 unless Host is `127.0.0.1:8767` / `localhost:8767`; 403 for a non-GET whose `Origin` isn't its own; no CORS headers. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Repo audit fixes]]
- `Development/sim/engine.py`: `staff` keys are lowercase everywhere; `on_answer(…, cashier)` (decided in `on_offer`); a till sale is added to `earned` at the sale (`task_till` only moves it to coins); `tend_tasks` racks only `spare` ingots (not `order_for`); `task_forge` checks `order_for` before the letters; crafter polish `level_seconds(…) * len(job["metals"])`; `best_bp` = `max(sorted(c), …)`; `purchases` rows `(minute, uid, price)`. `rules.etch_bonus` and `economy_model.py` were removed. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Repo audit fixes]]
- `Development/sim/report.py`: `series_minute(k)` is the one sample-to-minute rule (used by `time_model.income`); Income and its chart skip players with `horizon` < t; the gem sinks include `pads`. `run.summarize` gaps use `report.STUCK`. Tests: `test_engine` `test_cashier_and_promoter_work`, `test_tending_keeps_an_orders_ingot`, `test_crafter_polishes_each_sword`, `test_same_seed_any_hash_seed`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Repo audit fixes]]
- `Development/sim/engine.py` `on_offer` (2026-11-10): with the Cashier (`"cashier" in staff`, `sold >= 2`), the card's timeout is `B.perk("cashier", …)`. A missed card, or a player `delay >= timeout`, gets `cashier_sells(offer, value, None)` at the timeout with `cashier=True` (his till, his count). Otherwise it is the player's answer, `cashier=False`. This mirrors `ShopService.runCustomer` `askOwner` / `clerkAnswered`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Sim Cashier as the game has it]]

- `Development/sim/upgrade_tree/template.html` / `build_tree.py` (2026-11-10): the page lays out the tree itself (`layout()`), so the new Rewire mode can drag a pad onto its new prerequisite, re-lay columns, refuse loops, keep edits in localStorage and copy a change list (names + ids). `build_tree.py` only bakes data; default run `prices-recheck-2026-11-10`. Page: `Development/Upgrade Tree.html`, artifact UNFWP14KsNwNwFdJEpaQnD.

| `ServerScriptService.Analytics` | `CHAIN` from `Upgrades.list` (blueprint pads by `after`); kind `buy` also logs the SwordChain funnel step; kind `purchase` → custom event Purchase (Robux, key, Pass/Product, time bin). Requires `Shared/Upgrades`. |

| `WorldStoreLog` | The Shop funnel: `Remotes.ShopFunnel` ("Opened", "Prompted" + key), `visits[player]` (id = os.time(), pressed keys), Bought from both purchase-finished signals. Requires `Analytics`. |
| `ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.ShopFunnel` | New RemoteEvent, client → server, the Shop funnel's first two steps. |
| `MenuController` | Fires `ShopFunnel` "Opened" when `ShopWindow` shows and "Prompted" in `buy`. |

## Moving Secret metals (2026-11-10)

- `Shared/SecretFx` (new): `attach(part, fx, faces, path?)` adds a `Texture` per face and a `ParticleEmitter` (on an `Attachment` when a blade passes its edge path), all named `SecretFx`, off, and tags the part `SecretFx`; `clear(part)`; `paths[attachment]` = the blade's edge path (weak keys). `fx` fields: over, tile, move ("drift" / "rise" / "jump"), speed, sprite, colors, rate, size, life, lift, glow.
- `StarterPlayerScripts.SecretMetalFx` (new, client): tracks `SecretFx`-tagged parts, finds each one's `fx` from its MaterialVariant (`<texture>Blade` / `<texture>Ingot`), and at 30 Hz turns on the 6 nearest showing within 80 studs (NEAR, MAX, STEP): scrolls `OffsetStudsU` (minus = toward the tip), Lava Lamp's `Color3` shift, Glitch's random hops and flicker, the blade attachment's hop along its path.
- `Shared/Materials`: 45 metals; `fx` on Galaxy, Lava Lamp, Glitch (Secret); Watermelon and Rubber Duck in Epic, Cardboard in Rare; `dressIngot` calls `SecretFx.attach` / `clear`; CORNER_IDS for the three. `Material` type has `fx: any?`.
- `SwordRenderer`: after `edgePath`, `SecretFx.attach(part, material.fx, {Top, Bottom}, path)` for a metal with `fx`.
- `Shared/Showcase.FAMILY`: Galaxy Mail, Lava Lamp Coins, Glitch Swift. `Shared/Etches.MATERIAL_FLAME`: the three.
- `MaterialService`: GalaxyIngot / GalaxyBlade (Metal), LavaLampIngot / LavaLampBlade, GlitchIngot / GlitchBlade (Plastic).
- Assets: `Models/Metal/secret_metals.py` → `Art/Secret Metals`. Backups `Legacy.Materials_ / Showcase_ / Etches_beforeSecretRedo_1110`, `Legacy.SwordRenderer_beforeSecretFx_1110`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Moving Secret metals built]]
- Secret layers (2026-11-10): `fx.layers` = { tile, u (studs/s, minus toward the tip), wobble, wf, twinkle, tf, phase }; `SecretFx.attach` one Texture per layer per face (`Layer` attribute); `SecretMetalFx` `e.layer[texture]` moves each. Backups `Legacy.SecretFx_ / SecretMetalFx_beforeLayers_1110`. [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Secret metals: layered motion]]
- `CrewGui.Window.Staff.Rows.Setting.Hint` (2026-11-10): set by `EmployeeController.showPct` from the pick ("He takes every offer" / "Under +N%: he says no"); was a fixed "Lower offers: he asks for more". [[Sessions/2026-11-10#Cashier: slider hint and his gate]]

## Gifts (2026-11-10)

[[Sessions/2026-11-10#Gifts from the Shop]]. Ids and rules in [[Design/Monetization Plan#Gifts (2026-11-10)]].

| Where | What |
|---|---|
| `Shared/Store` | `GIFTS` (item key → "Gift: <item>" developer product; the 10 passes and 11 currency packs), `canGift(to, key)`: a gift key, and `to` owns neither the pass nor any pass in the bundle. |
| `ServerScriptService.Gifts` (new ModuleScript) | `Gifts.new(deps)` → `receipt(buyer, key, purchase)` (to the friend in this server and loaded, saving them first; else an entry `{id, key, from}` in their inbox list, `deps.queued(UserId)`; then the buyer's receipt and save) and `drain(player, valid)` (gives inbox entries not in their receipts, saves, removes what it read, keeps what arrived since). `INBOX` 100 (a full inbox errors: NotProcessedYet). |
| `ServerScriptService.StoreService` | `Remotes.Gift` (new RemoteEvent; key, UserId): 1 s cooldown per player, the checks, `giftTo[key]`, `PromptProductPurchase` from the server. `ProcessReceipt`: gift products go to `gifts.receipt`; the Purchase log key `Gift<key>`; notices on `Remotes.ShopNotice`. `giveGift`: a pass → `d.gifted` + `grant`; packs → `addGems` / `addCoins` (sku `Gift<key>`). `showStarter` (on load and Reset progress) also grants `d.gifted` and drains the inbox. Inbox store `Swords_Gifts_v1` (`_Studio_v1` in Studio), made on first use. The `PlayerAdded` hookup moved to the end of the script. |
| `ServerScriptService.SmithyCore` | `SmithData.gifted` ({ string }, ≤ 50) and `giftTo` ({ [key]: UserId }), saved; decode drops bad values. |

| `ForgeLabGui.Panel.Arm` | toggle at (348, 38); restarts the blade. Panel 486 px. |
| `AnvilController.arm` | one table (the script is at the 200-local limit): `held`, `goal` (hammerAt glides to it), `at`, `started` (was `labStarted`), `hits` (was `labHits`), parts `hand` / `fore` / `sleeve` (in `hammerModel` while held), `limb()`, `rest()`, `wind(last)`. `headOffset` / `contactRaise` / `placeHammer` switch to the elbow pivot and the mirrored angle when held. |

## FirstPull analytics (2026-11-12)

| `ServerScriptService.Analytics` | **FirstPull** custom event, once per visit of a save with no lever pull yet (onboarding funnel id 2 unset when `SmithyCore.waitLoaded` returns; `trackFirstPull` from PlayerAdded). `State.first` = `{ holds, reach, closest, done, prompt }`. `firstPull(player, outcome)` sends it: on the first `pull` log ("Pulled"), or in the `onLeave` hook ("Loading" without `loadedAt`, "Let go early" with holds, "In reach", "Never in reach"). `leverOf(player)` = the plot's `RollPrompt`. `ProximityPromptService.PromptButtonHoldBegan` counts holds on your own lever; a 1 s loop keeps `closest` (character root to the prompt's parent) until within `MaxActivationDistance`. `workspace.NewSwordGallery` (new silhouette swords, Edit viewing): its `StudioOnly` Script destroys the model whenever it runs, so live servers and Studio playtests never have it. `WallCap` parts (plot `Fence`, street `GapWall`s, `ServerStorage.WallPieces`) collide; the street/plot builders in `ServerStorage.Builders` still make them non-colliding. [[Sessions/2026-11-12#Sword gallery out of playtests; offer card, Starter price and wall caps in Edit]]

`MailGui.Window.RedHeader` (2026-11-13): flush header, anchor (0, 0) at y 0, 77 tall, with `Content.UIPadding` top 12; the other windows' RedHeaders sit centred on their top border instead (Deliveries is flush too).

### Auto-pass buttons (2026-11-13)

`StarterPlayerScripts.AutoPassButtons` paints `AutoRollGui.Bar.Button`, `AnvilHud.Auto`, `EtchHud.Auto` and `PolishHud.Auto` (each: `Art` 3D face, `Price` tilted tag, hidden `Icon`) from their controllers' own state: `Icon.Lock` visible means locked (green, name only, live pass price); else dark with the controller's "NAME: ON/OFF". The controllers (AutoRollController, AnvilController, EtchingTableController, PolishController) are unchanged in what they decide; PolishController reaches its button as `gui.Auto`. `AutoRollGui.Bar.Config.Drop` holds the window body (Shadow, Fill, ChecksClip) with the settings in `Drop.Rows`; its chevron and pickers use the HUD arrow image (`Glyph`). [[Sessions/2026-11-13#Auto-pass buttons, settings drop, Auto-Polish and picker Back ported to Edit]]
